// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
pragma solidity ^0.8.24;

import "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol";
import "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol";

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  Interfaces de DEXes en Base (ChainID 8453)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

interface IUniswapV3Router {
    struct ExactInputSingleParams {
        address tokenIn;
        address tokenOut;
        uint24 fee;
        address recipient;
        uint256 amountIn;
        uint256 amountOutMinimum;
        uint160 sqrtPriceLimitX96;
    }
    function exactInputSingle(ExactInputSingleParams calldata params) external payable returns (uint256 amountOut);
}

// Interfaz estándar de 8 parámetros (selector 0x414bf389) para PancakeSwap V3 y SushiSwap V3
interface IPancakeV3Router {
    struct ExactInputSingleParams {
        address tokenIn;
        address tokenOut;
        uint24 fee;
        address recipient;
        uint256 deadline;
        uint256 amountIn;
        uint256 amountOutMinimum;
        uint160 sqrtPriceLimitX96;
    }
    function exactInputSingle(ExactInputSingleParams calldata params) external payable returns (uint256 amountOut);
}

interface IAerodromeSlipstreamRouter {
    struct ExactInputSingleParams {
        address tokenIn;
        address tokenOut;
        int24 tickSpacing;
        address recipient;
        uint256 deadline;
        uint256 amountIn;
        uint256 amountOutMinimum;
        uint160 sqrtPriceLimitX96;
    }
    function exactInputSingle(ExactInputSingleParams calldata params) external payable returns (uint256 amountOut);
}

interface IAerodromeV2Router {
    struct Route {
        address from;
        address to;
        bool stable;
        address factory;
    }
    function swapExactTokensForTokens(
        uint256 amountIn,
        uint256 amountOutMin,
        Route[] calldata routes,
        address to,
        uint256 deadline
    ) external returns (uint256[] memory amounts);
}

interface IPool {
    function flashLoanSimple(
        address receiverAddress,
        address asset,
        uint256 amount,
        bytes calldata params,
        uint16 referralCode
    ) external;
}

interface IBalancerVault {
    function flashLoan(
        address recipient,
        IERC20[] memory tokens,
        uint256[] memory amounts,
        bytes memory userData
    ) external;
}

interface IFlashLoanRecipient {
    function receiveFlashLoan(
        IERC20[] memory tokens,
        uint256[] memory amounts,
        uint256[] memory feeAmounts,
        bytes memory userData
    ) external;
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  Contrato Universal de Arbitraje Dedicado a Flash Loans (Base)
//  100% Flash Loans (Balancer V2 0% Fee / Aave V3) - 0% Capital Propio
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

contract BaseUniversalArbitrage is IFlashLoanRecipient {
    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;

    address public owner;

    // ── Direcciones Inmutables en Base Mainnet (ChainID 8453) ──
    address public constant WETH = 0x4200000000000000000000000000000000000006;
    address public constant USDC = 0x833589fCD6eDb6E08f4c7C32D4f71b54bdA02913;

    IUniswapV3Router public constant UNIV3_ROUTER = IUniswapV3Router(0x2626664c2603336E57B271c5C0b26F421741e481);
    IAerodromeSlipstreamRouter public constant AERO_CL_ROUTER = IAerodromeSlipstreamRouter(0xBE6D8f0d05cC4be24d5167a3eF062215bE6D18a5);
    IAerodromeV2Router public constant AERO_V2_ROUTER = IAerodromeV2Router(0xcF77a3Ba9A5CA399B7c97c74d54e5b1Beb874E43);
    address public constant AERO_V2_FACTORY = 0x420DD381b31aEf6683db6B902084cB0FFECe40Da;
    IPancakeV3Router public constant PANCAKE_V3_ROUTER = IPancakeV3Router(0x1b81D678ffb9C0263b24A97847620C99d213eB14);
    IPancakeV3Router public constant SUSHISWAP_V3_ROUTER = IPancakeV3Router(0xFB7eF66a7e61224DD6FcD0D7d9C3be5C8B049b9f);
    IPool public constant AAVE_POOL = IPool(0xA238Dd80C259a72e81d7e4664a9801593F98d1c5);
    address public constant BALANCER_VAULT = 0xBA12222222228d8Ba445958a75a0704d566BF2C8;

    // ── Identificadores de DEX ──
    uint8 public constant DEX_UNIV3 = 0;
    uint8 public constant DEX_AERO_CL = 1;
    uint8 public constant DEX_AERO_V2_VOLATILE = 2;
    uint8 public constant DEX_AERO_V2_STABLE = 3;
    uint8 public constant DEX_PANCAKE_V3 = 4;
    uint8 public constant DEX_SUSHISWAP_V3 = 5;

    // ── Reentrancy Guard Gas-Efficient ──
    uint256 private constant _NOT_ENTERED = 1;
    uint256 private constant _ENTERED = 2;
    uint256 private _status = _NOT_ENTERED;

    // ── Variables de Retorno y Seguridad de Callbacks ──
    uint256 private _lastProfit;
    bool private _inFlashLoan;

    struct SpatialRoute {
        address tokenIn;
        address tokenIntermediate;
        uint8 dexBuy;
        uint8 dexSell;
        uint24 feeOrSpacingBuy;
        uint24 feeOrSpacingSell;
        uint256 minProfit;
    }

    struct TriangularRoute {
        address tokenA;
        address tokenB;
        address tokenC;
        uint8 dex1;
        uint8 dex2;
        uint8 dex3;
        uint24 feeOrSpacing1;
        uint24 feeOrSpacing2;
        uint24 feeOrSpacing3;
        uint256 minProfit;
    }

    // ── Errores Personalizados ──
    error NotOwner();
    error NotProfitable(uint256 received, uint256 required);
    error Reentrancy();
    error SwapFailed(uint8 dex);
    error Unauthorized();

    // ── Evento Oficial de Flash Loan ──
    event FlashLoanExecuted(address indexed token, uint256 loanAmount, uint256 grossProfit, uint256 timestamp);

    modifier onlyOwner() {
        if (msg.sender != owner) revert NotOwner();
        _;
    }

    modifier nonReentrant() {
        if (_status == _ENTERED) revert Reentrancy();
        _status = _ENTERED;
        _;
        _status = _NOT_ENTERED;
    }

    constructor() {
        owner = msg.sender;
    }

    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  1. Flash Loans con Aave V3 (0% Capital Propio)
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    function executeFlashLoanSpatial(SpatialRoute calldata route, uint256 loanAmount) external onlyOwner nonReentrant returns (uint256 profit) {
        _inFlashLoan = true;
        _lastProfit = 0;
        bytes memory params = abi.encode(uint8(2), abi.encode(route));
        AAVE_POOL.flashLoanSimple(address(this), route.tokenIn, loanAmount, params, 0);
        _inFlashLoan = false;
        return _lastProfit;
    }

    function executeFlashLoanTriangular(TriangularRoute calldata route, uint256 loanAmount) external onlyOwner nonReentrant returns (uint256 profit) {
        _inFlashLoan = true;
        _lastProfit = 0;
        bytes memory params = abi.encode(uint8(3), abi.encode(route));
        AAVE_POOL.flashLoanSimple(address(this), route.tokenA, loanAmount, params, 0);
        _inFlashLoan = false;
        return _lastProfit;
    }

    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  2. Flash Loans con Balancer V2 (0% Fee - 0% Capital Propio)
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    function executeBalancerFlashLoanSpatial(SpatialRoute calldata route, uint256 loanAmount) external onlyOwner nonReentrant returns (uint256 profit) {
        _inFlashLoan = true;
        _lastProfit = 0;
        IERC20[] memory tokens = new IERC20[](1);
        tokens[0] = IERC20(route.tokenIn);
        uint256[] memory amounts = new uint256[](1);
        amounts[0] = loanAmount;
        bytes memory userData = abi.encode(uint8(2), abi.encode(route));
        IBalancerVault(BALANCER_VAULT).flashLoan(address(this), tokens, amounts, userData);
        _inFlashLoan = false;
        return _lastProfit;
    }

    function executeBalancerFlashLoanTriangular(TriangularRoute calldata route, uint256 loanAmount) external onlyOwner nonReentrant returns (uint256 profit) {
        _inFlashLoan = true;
        _lastProfit = 0;
        IERC20[] memory tokens = new IERC20[](1);
        tokens[0] = IERC20(route.tokenA);
        uint256[] memory amounts = new uint256[](1);
        amounts[0] = loanAmount;
        bytes memory userData = abi.encode(uint8(3), abi.encode(route));
        IBalancerVault(BALANCER_VAULT).flashLoan(address(this), tokens, amounts, userData);
        _inFlashLoan = false;
        return _lastProfit;
    }

    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  3. Callback Balancer V2 con AISLAMIENTO DE SALDO PREVIO
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    function receiveFlashLoan(
        IERC20[] memory tokens,
        uint256[] memory amounts,
        uint256[] memory feeAmounts,
        bytes memory userData
    ) external override {
        require(msg.sender == BALANCER_VAULT, "Caller not Balancer Vault");
        require(_inFlashLoan, "Unauthorized flash loan initiation");

        address asset = address(tokens[0]);
        uint256 amount = amounts[0];
        uint256 amountOwed = amount + feeAmounts[0]; // Balancer fee is 0%

        // AISLAMIENTO DE CAPITAL: Capturar saldo previo a la llegada del préstamo
        uint256 currentBalAtEntry = IERC20(asset).balanceOf(address(this));
        uint256 preExistingBalance = currentBalAtEntry > amount ? currentBalAtEntry - amount : 0;

        (uint8 mode, bytes memory routeData) = abi.decode(userData, (uint8, bytes));
        uint256 minProfit;

        if (mode == 2) {
            SpatialRoute memory route = abi.decode(routeData, (SpatialRoute));
            minProfit = route.minProfit;
            uint256 mid = _swap(route.dexBuy, asset, route.tokenIntermediate, amount, route.feeOrSpacingBuy);
            _swap(route.dexSell, route.tokenIntermediate, asset, mid, route.feeOrSpacingSell);
        } else {
            TriangularRoute memory route = abi.decode(routeData, (TriangularRoute));
            minProfit = route.minProfit;
            uint256 out1 = _swap(route.dex1, asset, route.tokenB, amount, route.feeOrSpacing1);
            uint256 out2 = _swap(route.dex2, route.tokenB, route.tokenC, out1, route.feeOrSpacing2);
            _swap(route.dex3, route.tokenC, asset, out2, route.feeOrSpacing3);
        }

        uint256 currentBalance = IERC20(asset).balanceOf(address(this));
        uint256 requiredBalance = preExistingBalance + amountOwed + minProfit;

        // Prohibido subsidiar operaciones fallidas con saldo previo
        if (currentBalance < requiredBalance) {
            revert NotProfitable(currentBalance, requiredBalance);
        }

        IERC20(asset).safeTransfer(BALANCER_VAULT, amountOwed);
        uint256 profit = currentBalance - amountOwed - preExistingBalance;
        _lastProfit = profit;

        if (profit > 0) {
            IERC20(asset).safeTransfer(owner, profit);
        }

        emit FlashLoanExecuted(asset, amount, profit, block.timestamp);
    }

    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  4. Callback Aave V3 con AISLAMIENTO DE SALDO PREVIO
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    function executeOperation(
        address asset,
        uint256 amount,
        uint256 premium,
        address initiator,
        bytes calldata params
    ) external returns (bool) {
        require(msg.sender == address(AAVE_POOL), "Invalid caller");
        require(initiator == address(this), "Invalid initiator");
        require(_inFlashLoan, "Unauthorized flash loan initiation");

        uint256 totalOwed = amount + premium;

        // AISLAMIENTO DE CAPITAL: Capturar saldo previo a la llegada del préstamo
        uint256 currentBalAtEntry = IERC20(asset).balanceOf(address(this));
        uint256 preExistingBalance = currentBalAtEntry > amount ? currentBalAtEntry - amount : 0;

        (uint8 mode, bytes memory routeData) = abi.decode(params, (uint8, bytes));
        uint256 minProfit;

        if (mode == 2) {
            SpatialRoute memory route = abi.decode(routeData, (SpatialRoute));
            minProfit = route.minProfit;
            uint256 mid = _swap(route.dexBuy, asset, route.tokenIntermediate, amount, route.feeOrSpacingBuy);
            _swap(route.dexSell, route.tokenIntermediate, asset, mid, route.feeOrSpacingSell);
        } else {
            TriangularRoute memory route = abi.decode(routeData, (TriangularRoute));
            minProfit = route.minProfit;
            uint256 out1 = _swap(route.dex1, asset, route.tokenB, amount, route.feeOrSpacing1);
            uint256 out2 = _swap(route.dex2, route.tokenB, route.tokenC, out1, route.feeOrSpacing2);
            _swap(route.dex3, route.tokenC, asset, out2, route.feeOrSpacing3);
        }

        uint256 currentBalance = IERC20(asset).balanceOf(address(this));
        uint256 requiredBalance = preExistingBalance + totalOwed + minProfit;

        // Prohibido subsidiar operaciones perdedoras con saldo previo
        if (currentBalance < requiredBalance) {
            revert NotProfitable(currentBalance, requiredBalance);
        }

        IERC20(asset).forceApprove(address(AAVE_POOL), totalOwed);
        uint256 profit = currentBalance - totalOwed - preExistingBalance;
        _lastProfit = profit;

        if (profit > 0) {
            IERC20(asset).safeTransfer(owner, profit);
        }

        emit FlashLoanExecuted(asset, amount, profit, block.timestamp);
        return true;
    }

    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  Motor Interno de Enrutamiento Multi-DEX Corregido
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    function _swap(
        uint8 dex,
        address tokenIn,
        address tokenOut,
        uint256 amountIn,
        uint24 feeOrSpacing
    ) internal returns (uint256 amountOut) {
        if (dex == DEX_UNIV3) {
            _ensureAllowance(tokenIn, address(UNIV3_ROUTER), amountIn);
            amountOut = UNIV3_ROUTER.exactInputSingle(IUniswapV3Router.ExactInputSingleParams({
                tokenIn: tokenIn,
                tokenOut: tokenOut,
                fee: feeOrSpacing,
                recipient: address(this),
                amountIn: amountIn,
                amountOutMinimum: 0,
                sqrtPriceLimitX96: 0
            }));
        } else if (dex == DEX_AERO_CL) {
            _ensureAllowance(tokenIn, address(AERO_CL_ROUTER), amountIn);
            amountOut = AERO_CL_ROUTER.exactInputSingle(IAerodromeSlipstreamRouter.ExactInputSingleParams({
                tokenIn: tokenIn,
                tokenOut: tokenOut,
                tickSpacing: int24(uint24(feeOrSpacing)),
                recipient: address(this),
                deadline: block.timestamp + 120,
                amountIn: amountIn,
                amountOutMinimum: 0,
                sqrtPriceLimitX96: 0
            }));
        } else if (dex == DEX_AERO_V2_VOLATILE || dex == DEX_AERO_V2_STABLE) {
            _ensureAllowance(tokenIn, address(AERO_V2_ROUTER), amountIn);
            bool isStable = (dex == DEX_AERO_V2_STABLE);
            IAerodromeV2Router.Route[] memory routes = new IAerodromeV2Router.Route[](1);
            routes[0] = IAerodromeV2Router.Route({
                from: tokenIn,
                to: tokenOut,
                stable: isStable,
                factory: AERO_V2_FACTORY
            });
            uint256[] memory amounts = AERO_V2_ROUTER.swapExactTokensForTokens(
                amountIn,
                0,
                routes,
                address(this),
                block.timestamp + 120
            );
            amountOut = amounts[amounts.length - 1];
        } else if (dex == DEX_PANCAKE_V3) {
            _ensureAllowance(tokenIn, address(PANCAKE_V3_ROUTER), amountIn);
            // PancakeSwap V3 exige estructura de 8 parámetros con deadline (selector 0x414bf389)
            amountOut = PANCAKE_V3_ROUTER.exactInputSingle(IPancakeV3Router.ExactInputSingleParams({
                tokenIn: tokenIn,
                tokenOut: tokenOut,
                fee: feeOrSpacing,
                recipient: address(this),
                deadline: block.timestamp + 120,
                amountIn: amountIn,
                amountOutMinimum: 0,
                sqrtPriceLimitX96: 0
            }));
        } else if (dex == DEX_SUSHISWAP_V3) {
            _ensureAllowance(tokenIn, address(SUSHISWAP_V3_ROUTER), amountIn);
            // SushiSwap V3 exige estructura de 8 parámetros con deadline (selector 0x414bf389)
            amountOut = SUSHISWAP_V3_ROUTER.exactInputSingle(IPancakeV3Router.ExactInputSingleParams({
                tokenIn: tokenIn,
                tokenOut: tokenOut,
                fee: feeOrSpacing,
                recipient: address(this),
                deadline: block.timestamp + 120,
                amountIn: amountIn,
                amountOutMinimum: 0,
                sqrtPriceLimitX96: 0
            }));
        } else {
            revert SwapFailed(dex);
        }

        if (amountOut == 0) revert SwapFailed(dex);
    }

    function _ensureAllowance(address token, address spender, uint256 amount) internal {
        if (IERC20(token).allowance(address(this), spender) < amount) {
            IERC20(token).forceApprove(spender, type(uint256).max);
        }
    }

    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  Administración de Fondos
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    function approveRouters(address[] calldata tokens) external onlyOwner {
        for (uint256 i = 0; i < tokens.length; i++) {
            IERC20(tokens[i]).forceApprove(address(UNIV3_ROUTER), type(uint256).max);
            IERC20(tokens[i]).forceApprove(address(AERO_CL_ROUTER), type(uint256).max);
            IERC20(tokens[i]).forceApprove(address(AERO_V2_ROUTER), type(uint256).max);
            IERC20(tokens[i]).forceApprove(address(PANCAKE_V3_ROUTER), type(uint256).max);
            IERC20(tokens[i]).forceApprove(address(SUSHISWAP_V3_ROUTER), type(uint256).max);
        }
    }

    function withdrawToken(address token) external onlyOwner {
        uint256 bal = IERC20(token).balanceOf(address(this));
        if (bal > 0) IERC20(token).safeTransfer(owner, bal);
    }

    function withdrawETH() external onlyOwner {
        uint256 bal = address(this).balance;
        if (bal > 0) {
            (bool ok, ) = owner.call{value: bal}("");
            require(ok, "ETH transfer failed");
        }
    }

    receive() external payable {}
}

// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT
pragma solidity ^0.8.24;

import "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/IERC20.sol";
import "@openzeppelin/contracts/token/ERC20/utils/SafeERC20.sol";

// Interfaces de DEX Routers en Arbitrum One
interface IUniswapV3Router {
    struct ExactInputSingleParams {
        address tokenIn;
        address tokenOut;
        uint24 fee;
        address recipient;
        uint256 amountIn;
        uint256 amountOutMinimum;
        uint160 sqrtPriceLimitX96;
    }
    function exactInputSingle(ExactInputSingleParams calldata params) external payable returns (uint256 amountOut);
}

// Interfaz estándar de 8 parámetros (selector 0x414bf389) para PancakeSwap V3 y SushiSwap V3
interface IPancakeV3Router {
    struct ExactInputSingleParams {
        address tokenIn;
        address tokenOut;
        uint24 fee;
        address recipient;
        uint256 deadline;
        uint256 amountIn;
        uint256 amountOutMinimum;
        uint160 sqrtPriceLimitX96;
    }
    function exactInputSingle(ExactInputSingleParams calldata params) external payable returns (uint256 amountOut);
}

interface ICamelotV3Router {
    struct ExactInputSingleParams {
        address tokenIn;
        address tokenOut;
        address recipient;
        uint256 deadline;
        uint256 amountIn;
        uint256 amountOutMinimum;
        uint160 limitSqrtPrice;
    }
    function exactInputSingle(ExactInputSingleParams calldata params) external payable returns (uint256 amountOut);
}

interface ICamelotV2Router {
    function swapExactTokensForTokens(
        uint256 amountIn,
        uint256 amountOutMin,
        address[] calldata path,
        address to,
        uint256 deadline
    ) external returns (uint256[] memory amounts);
}

// Aave V3 Flash Loan Interfaces
interface IFlashLoanSimpleReceiver {
    function executeOperation(
        address asset,
        uint256 amount,
        uint256 premium,
        address initiator,
        bytes calldata params
    ) external returns (bool);
}

interface IPool {
    function flashLoanSimple(
        address receiverAddress,
        address asset,
        uint256 amount,
        bytes calldata params,
        uint16 referralCode
    ) external;
}

// Balancer V2 Flash Loan Interfaces (0% Fee)
interface IBalancerVault {
    function flashLoan(
        address recipient,
        IERC20[] memory tokens,
        uint256[] memory amounts,
        bytes memory userData
    ) external;
}

interface IFlashLoanRecipient {
    function receiveFlashLoan(
        IERC20[] memory tokens,
        uint256[] memory amounts,
        uint256[] memory feeAmounts,
        bytes memory userData
    ) external;
}

contract ArbitrumUniversalArbitrage is IFlashLoanSimpleReceiver, IFlashLoanRecipient {
    using SafeERC20 for IERC20;

    // Constantes de Red en Arbitrum One (ChainID 42161)
    address public constant WETH = 0x82aF49447D8a07e3bd95BD0d56f35241523fBab1;
    address public constant AAVE_POOL = 0x794a61358D6845594F94dc1DB02A252b5b4814aD;
    address public constant BALANCER_VAULT = 0xBA12222222228d8Ba445958a75a0704d566BF2C8;

    // DEX Routers en Arbitrum One
    address public constant UNIV3_ROUTER = 0x68b3465833fb72A70ecDF485E0e4C7bD8665Fc45;
    address public constant CAMELOT_V3_ROUTER = 0x1F721E2E82F6676FCE4eA07A5958cF098D339e18;
    address public constant CAMELOT_V2_ROUTER = 0xc873fEcbd354f5A56E00E710B90EF4201db2448d;
    address public constant PANCAKE_V3_ROUTER = 0x1b81D678ffb9C0263b24A97847620C99d213eB14;
    // Dirección oficial de SushiSwap V3 Router en Arbitrum One
    address public constant SUSHISWAP_V3_ROUTER = 0x8A21F6768C1f8075791D08546Dadf6daA0bE820c;

    // Enum de DEXes en Arbitrum
    enum DexType {
        UNIV3,          // 0: Uniswap V3 (Concentrated Liquidity)
        CAMELOT_V3,     // 1: Camelot V3 / Algebra (CL)
        CAMELOT_V2,     // 2: Camelot Classic AMM V2
        PANCAKE_V3,     // 3: PancakeSwap V3 (CL - 8 params)
        SUSHISWAP_V3    // 4: SushiSwap V3 (CL - 8 params)
    }

    struct SpatialRoute {
        address tokenIn;
        address tokenIntermediate;
        uint8 dexBuy;
        uint8 dexSell;
        uint24 feeOrSpacingBuy;
        uint24 feeOrSpacingSell;
        uint256 minProfit;
    }

    struct TriangularRoute {
        address tokenA;
        address tokenB;
        address tokenC;
        uint8 dex1;
        uint8 dex2;
        uint8 dex3;
        uint24 feeOrSpacing1;
        uint24 feeOrSpacing2;
        uint24 feeOrSpacing3;
        uint256 minProfit;
    }

    address public owner;

    // ── Variables de Retorno y Seguridad de Callbacks ──
    uint256 private _lastProfit;
    bool private _inFlashLoan;

    event FlashLoanExecuted(address indexed token, uint256 loanAmount, uint256 grossProfit, uint256 timestamp);

    error OnlyOwner();
    error NotAavePool();
    error NotBalancerVault();
    error NotProfitable(uint256 received, uint256 required);
    error InvalidDEX(uint8 dexId);

    modifier onlyOwner() {
        if (msg.sender != owner) revert OnlyOwner();
        _;
    }

    constructor() {
        owner = msg.sender;
    }

    receive() external payable {}

    function _approveTokenToRouters(address token) internal {
        IERC20(token).forceApprove(UNIV3_ROUTER, type(uint256).max);
        IERC20(token).forceApprove(CAMELOT_V3_ROUTER, type(uint256).max);
        IERC20(token).forceApprove(CAMELOT_V2_ROUTER, type(uint256).max);
        IERC20(token).forceApprove(PANCAKE_V3_ROUTER, type(uint256).max);
        IERC20(token).forceApprove(SUSHISWAP_V3_ROUTER, type(uint256).max);
    }

    function approveRouters(address[] calldata tokens) external onlyOwner {
        for (uint256 i = 0; i < tokens.length; i++) {
            _approveTokenToRouters(tokens[i]);
        }
    }

    function _swap(
        address tokenIn,
        address tokenOut,
        uint256 amountIn,
        uint8 dexId,
        uint24 feeOrSpacing
    ) internal returns (uint256 amountOut) {
        address router;
        if (dexId == uint8(DexType.UNIV3)) router = UNIV3_ROUTER;
        else if (dexId == uint8(DexType.CAMELOT_V3)) router = CAMELOT_V3_ROUTER;
        else if (dexId == uint8(DexType.CAMELOT_V2)) router = CAMELOT_V2_ROUTER;
        else if (dexId == uint8(DexType.PANCAKE_V3)) router = PANCAKE_V3_ROUTER;
        else if (dexId == uint8(DexType.SUSHISWAP_V3)) router = SUSHISWAP_V3_ROUTER;
        else revert InvalidDEX(dexId);

        if (IERC20(tokenIn).allowance(address(this), router) < amountIn) {
            IERC20(tokenIn).forceApprove(router, type(uint256).max);
        }

        if (dexId == uint8(DexType.UNIV3)) {
            IUniswapV3Router.ExactInputSingleParams memory params = IUniswapV3Router.ExactInputSingleParams({
                tokenIn: tokenIn,
                tokenOut: tokenOut,
                fee: feeOrSpacing,
                recipient: address(this),
                amountIn: amountIn,
                amountOutMinimum: 0,
                sqrtPriceLimitX96: 0
            });
            return IUniswapV3Router(router).exactInputSingle(params);
        } else if (dexId == uint8(DexType.PANCAKE_V3) || dexId == uint8(DexType.SUSHISWAP_V3)) {
            // Variante de 8 parámetros con deadline para PancakeSwap V3 y SushiSwap V3 (selector 0x414bf389)
            IPancakeV3Router.ExactInputSingleParams memory params = IPancakeV3Router.ExactInputSingleParams({
                tokenIn: tokenIn,
                tokenOut: tokenOut,
                fee: feeOrSpacing,
                recipient: address(this),
                deadline: block.timestamp + 120,
                amountIn: amountIn,
                amountOutMinimum: 0,
                sqrtPriceLimitX96: 0
            });
            return IPancakeV3Router(router).exactInputSingle(params);
        } else if (dexId == uint8(DexType.CAMELOT_V3)) {
            ICamelotV3Router.ExactInputSingleParams memory params = ICamelotV3Router.ExactInputSingleParams({
                tokenIn: tokenIn,
                tokenOut: tokenOut,
                recipient: address(this),
                deadline: block.timestamp + 120,
                amountIn: amountIn,
                amountOutMinimum: 0,
                limitSqrtPrice: 0
            });
            return ICamelotV3Router(router).exactInputSingle(params);
        } else if (dexId == uint8(DexType.CAMELOT_V2)) {
            address[] memory path = new address[](2);
            path[0] = tokenIn;
            path[1] = tokenOut;
            uint256[] memory amounts = ICamelotV2Router(router).swapExactTokensForTokens(
                amountIn,
                0,
                path,
                address(this),
                block.timestamp + 120
            );
            return amounts[amounts.length - 1];
        }
        revert InvalidDEX(dexId);
    }

    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  1. Flash Loans con Aave V3 (Arbitrum)
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    function executeFlashLoanSpatial(SpatialRoute calldata route, uint256 loanAmount) external onlyOwner returns (uint256 profit) {
        _inFlashLoan = true;
        _lastProfit = 0;
        bytes memory params = abi.encode(uint8(0), abi.encode(route));
        IPool(AAVE_POOL).flashLoanSimple(address(this), route.tokenIn, loanAmount, params, 0);
        _inFlashLoan = false;
        return _lastProfit;
    }

    function executeFlashLoanTriangular(TriangularRoute calldata route, uint256 loanAmount) external onlyOwner returns (uint256 profit) {
        _inFlashLoan = true;
        _lastProfit = 0;
        bytes memory params = abi.encode(uint8(1), abi.encode(route));
        IPool(AAVE_POOL).flashLoanSimple(address(this), route.tokenA, loanAmount, params, 0);
        _inFlashLoan = false;
        return _lastProfit;
    }

    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  2. Flash Loans con Balancer V2 (Arbitrum - 0% Fee)
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    function executeBalancerFlashLoanSpatial(SpatialRoute calldata route, uint256 loanAmount) external onlyOwner returns (uint256 profit) {
        _inFlashLoan = true;
        _lastProfit = 0;
        IERC20[] memory tokens = new IERC20[](1);
        tokens[0] = IERC20(route.tokenIn);
        uint256[] memory amounts = new uint256[](1);
        amounts[0] = loanAmount;
        bytes memory userData = abi.encode(uint8(0), abi.encode(route));
        IBalancerVault(BALANCER_VAULT).flashLoan(address(this), tokens, amounts, userData);
        _inFlashLoan = false;
        return _lastProfit;
    }

    function executeBalancerFlashLoanTriangular(TriangularRoute calldata route, uint256 loanAmount) external onlyOwner returns (uint256 profit) {
        _inFlashLoan = true;
        _lastProfit = 0;
        IERC20[] memory tokens = new IERC20[](1);
        tokens[0] = IERC20(route.tokenA);
        uint256[] memory amounts = new uint256[](1);
        amounts[0] = loanAmount;
        bytes memory userData = abi.encode(uint8(1), abi.encode(route));
        IBalancerVault(BALANCER_VAULT).flashLoan(address(this), tokens, amounts, userData);
        _inFlashLoan = false;
        return _lastProfit;
    }

    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  3. Callback Aave V3 con AISLAMIENTO DE SALDO PREVIO
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    function executeOperation(
        address asset,
        uint256 amount,
        uint256 premium,
        address initiator,
        bytes calldata params
    ) external override returns (bool) {
        if (msg.sender != AAVE_POOL) revert NotAavePool();
        require(initiator == address(this), "Initiator not this contract");
        require(_inFlashLoan, "Unauthorized flash loan initiation");

        uint256 totalOwed = amount + premium;

        // AISLAMIENTO DE CAPITAL: Capturar saldo previo a la llegada del préstamo
        uint256 currentBalAtEntry = IERC20(asset).balanceOf(address(this));
        uint256 preExistingBalance = currentBalAtEntry > amount ? currentBalAtEntry - amount : 0;

        (uint8 routeType, bytes memory routeData) = abi.decode(params, (uint8, bytes));
        uint256 minProfit;

        if (routeType == 0) {
            SpatialRoute memory route = abi.decode(routeData, (SpatialRoute));
            minProfit = route.minProfit;
            uint256 interAmount = _swap(route.tokenIn, route.tokenIntermediate, amount, route.dexBuy, route.feeOrSpacingBuy);
            _swap(route.tokenIntermediate, route.tokenIn, interAmount, route.dexSell, route.feeOrSpacingSell);
        } else if (routeType == 1) {
            TriangularRoute memory route = abi.decode(routeData, (TriangularRoute));
            minProfit = route.minProfit;
            uint256 amountB = _swap(route.tokenA, route.tokenB, amount, route.dex1, route.feeOrSpacing1);
            uint256 amountC = _swap(route.tokenB, route.tokenC, amountB, route.dex2, route.feeOrSpacing2);
            _swap(route.tokenC, route.tokenA, amountC, route.dex3, route.feeOrSpacing3);
        }

        uint256 currentBalance = IERC20(asset).balanceOf(address(this));
        uint256 requiredBalance = preExistingBalance + totalOwed + minProfit;

        // Prohibido subsidiar operaciones perdedoras con saldo previo
        if (currentBalance < requiredBalance) {
            revert NotProfitable(currentBalance, requiredBalance);
        }

        IERC20(asset).forceApprove(AAVE_POOL, totalOwed);
        uint256 netProfit = currentBalance - totalOwed - preExistingBalance;
        _lastProfit = netProfit;

        if (netProfit > 0) {
            IERC20(asset).safeTransfer(owner, netProfit);
        }

        emit FlashLoanExecuted(asset, amount, netProfit, block.timestamp);
        return true;
    }

    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    //  4. Callback Balancer V2 con AISLAMIENTO DE SALDO PREVIO
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    function receiveFlashLoan(
        IERC20[] memory tokens,
        uint256[] memory amounts,
        uint256[] memory feeAmounts,
        bytes memory userData
    ) external override {
        if (msg.sender != BALANCER_VAULT) revert NotBalancerVault();
        require(_inFlashLoan, "Unauthorized flash loan initiation");

        address asset = address(tokens[0]);
        uint256 amount = amounts[0];
        uint256 amountOwed = amount + feeAmounts[0]; // Balancer fee is 0%

        // AISLAMIENTO DE CAPITAL: Capturar saldo previo a la llegada del préstamo
        uint256 currentBalAtEntry = IERC20(asset).balanceOf(address(this));
        uint256 preExistingBalance = currentBalAtEntry > amount ? currentBalAtEntry - amount : 0;

        (uint8 routeType, bytes memory routeData) = abi.decode(userData, (uint8, bytes));
        uint256 minProfit;

        if (routeType == 0) {
            SpatialRoute memory route = abi.decode(routeData, (SpatialRoute));
            minProfit = route.minProfit;
            uint256 interAmount = _swap(route.tokenIn, route.tokenIntermediate, amount, route.dexBuy, route.feeOrSpacingBuy);
            _swap(route.tokenIntermediate, route.tokenIn, interAmount, route.dexSell, route.feeOrSpacingSell);
        } else if (routeType == 1) {
            TriangularRoute memory route = abi.decode(routeData, (TriangularRoute));
            minProfit = route.minProfit;
            uint256 amountB = _swap(route.tokenA, route.tokenB, amount, route.dex1, route.feeOrSpacing1);
            uint256 amountC = _swap(route.tokenB, route.tokenC, amountB, route.dex2, route.feeOrSpacing2);
            _swap(route.tokenC, route.tokenA, amountC, route.dex3, route.feeOrSpacing3);
        }

        uint256 currentBalance = IERC20(asset).balanceOf(address(this));
        uint256 requiredBalance = preExistingBalance + amountOwed + minProfit;

        // Prohibido subsidiar operaciones perdedoras con saldo previo
        if (currentBalance < requiredBalance) {
            revert NotProfitable(currentBalance, requiredBalance);
        }

        IERC20(asset).safeTransfer(BALANCER_VAULT, amountOwed);
        uint256 netProfit = currentBalance - amountOwed - preExistingBalance;
        _lastProfit = netProfit;

        if (netProfit > 0) {
            IERC20(asset).safeTransfer(owner, netProfit);
        }

        emit FlashLoanExecuted(asset, amount, netProfit, block.timestamp);
    }

    function withdrawToken(address token) external onlyOwner {
        uint256 balance = IERC20(token).balanceOf(address(this));
        if (balance > 0) {
            IERC20(token).safeTransfer(owner, balance);
        }
    }

    function withdrawETH() external onlyOwner {
        uint256 balance = address(this).balance;
        if (balance > 0) {
            (bool s, ) = payable(owner).call{value: balance}("");
            require(s, "ETH Transfer failed");
        }
    }
}

import dotenv from 'dotenv';

dotenv.config();

export function parseDryRun(rawValue?: string): boolean {
  if (rawValue === undefined || rawValue === null) return true;
  const normalized = rawValue.trim().toLowerCase();
  if (normalized === 'false') return false;
  return true;
}

export function normalizeFlashLoanProvider(rawValue?: string): 'balancer' | 'aave' {
  const val = (rawValue || 'balancer').trim().toLowerCase();
  if (val === 'aave' || val === 'aavev3') return 'aave';
  return 'balancer';
}

  scanIntervalMs: parseInt(process.env.SCAN_INTERVAL_MS || '1000', 10),

export interface TokenConfig {
  chainId: number;
  address: string;
  decimals: number;
  symbol: string;
  name: string;
}

export interface ArbitragePair {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  chainId: number;
  tokenA: TokenConfig;
  tokenB: TokenConfig;
  defaultLoanAmount: string;
  approxPriceUSD: number;
  cexSymbol?: string;

}

export interface AgentDefinition {
  id: number;
  regimentId: number;
  name: string;
  role: string;
  targetChains: number[];

}

}

export function getAgentByIndex(id: number): AgentDefinition {
  const regimentIndex = Math.floor((id - 1) / 100) % REGIMENTS_METADATA.length;
  const regimentId = Math.floor((id - 1) / 100) + 1;
  const meta = REGIMENTS_METADATA[regimentIndex];
  const paddedId = id.toString().padStart(6, '0');

  return {
    id,
    regimentId,
    name: `Agent-${paddedId} (R${regimentId})`,
    role: `${meta.role} #${((id - 1) % 100) + 1}`,
    targetChains: meta.chain,
    color: meta.color,
  };
}

export function getRegimentAgents(regimentId: number): AgentDefinition[] {
  const list: AgentDefinition[] = [];
  const startId = (regimentId - 1) * 100 + 1;
  const endId = Math.min(regimentId * 100, TOTAL_AGENTS_COUNT);
  for (let i = startId; i <= endId; i++) {
    list.push(getAgentByIndex(i));
  }
  return list;
}

export function generate250000SwarmAgents(): AgentDefinition[] {
  const agents: AgentDefinition[] = [];
  // Precargar una muestra representativa en memoria
  for (let i = 1; i <= 2500; i++) {
    agents.push(getAgentByIndex(i));
  }
  return agents;
}

export const SWARM_AGENTS: AgentDefinition[] = generate250000SwarmAgents();

const UNISWAP_V3_FACTORY = '0x1F98431c8aD98523631AE4a59f267346ea31F984';
const UNISWAP_V3_INIT_CODE_HASH = '0xe34f199b19b2b4f47f68442619d555527d244f78a32977f00d33e0892040b016';

export function computeUniV3PoolAddress(
  tokenA: string,
  tokenB: string,
  fee: number,
  factory: string = UNISWAP_V3_FACTORY,
  initCodeHash: string = UNISWAP_V3_INIT_CODE_HASH
): string {
  const [token0, token1] = tokenA.toLowerCase() < tokenB.toLowerCase() ? [tokenA, tokenB] : [tokenB, tokenA];
  const salt = ethers.solidityPackedKeccak256(
    ['address', 'address', 'uint24'],
    [token0, token1, fee]
  );
  return ethers.getCreate2Address(factory, salt, initCodeHash);
}

export function getKnownPoolAddress(chainId: number, token0Symbol: string, token1Symbol: string, feeTier: number = 500): string | null {
  const key1 = `${chainId}-${token0Symbol}-${token1Symbol}`;
  const key2 = `${chainId}-${token1Symbol}-${token0Symbol}`;

  const list = KNOWN_ONCHAIN_POOLS[key1] || KNOWN_ONCHAIN_POOLS[key2];
  if (list) {
    const match = list.find(e => e.fee === feeTier) || list[0];
    if (match) return match.address;
  }

  return null;
}

███████████████████████████████████████████████████████████████████████████████
██ ARCHIVO: src/math/sweetSpotOptimizer.ts
██ RUTA ABSOLUTA: C:\Users\Jose\Desktop\PELI_TRIAUDIO\flashloan-arbitrage-agent-v2\src\math\sweetSpotOptimizer.ts
███████████████████████████████████████████████████████████████████████████████

import { ArbitragePair } from '../config/registry';
import { ArbitrageOpportunity } from '../protocolink/protocolinkClient';

export interface SweetSpotResult {
  optimalLoanAmount: string;
  optimalLoanUSD: number;
  expectedGrossProfitUSD: number;
  expectedNetProfitUSD: number;
  priceImpactPercent: number;
  sweetSpotRatio: number;
  isViable: boolean;
  l1DataFeeUSD?: number;
  l2ExecutionGasUSD?: number;
}

export class SweetSpotOptimizer {
  private static PHI = (1 + Math.sqrt(5)) / 2; // Golden Ratio (~1.6180339887)
  private static RES_PHI = 2 - SweetSpotOptimizer.PHI; // (~0.3819660113)

  // Live L1 Fee Cache (EIP-4844 Blobs & BaseFee)
  public static liveL1BaseFeeBase: bigint = 62000000n; // ~0.062 gwei in wei
  public static liveBlobBaseFeeBase: bigint = 3800000n; // ~0.0038 gwei in wei
  public static liveBaseFeeScalarBase: number = 2269;
  public static liveBlobBaseFeeScalarBase: number = 1055762;
  public static liveL1BaseFeeArb: bigint = 1200000n; // ~0.0012 gwei in wei
  public static lastL1UpdateTimestamp: number = 0;
  private static isUpdatingL1Fees: boolean = false;

  /**
   * Actualiza los parámetros en tiempo real de L1 Data Fee desde los oráculos oficiales on-chain:
   * - Base: GasPriceOracle en 0x420000000000000000000000000000000000000F
   * - Arbitrum: ArbGasInfo precompile en 0x000000000000000000000000000000000000006C
   */
  public static async updateL1GasFees(providerBase?: any, providerArb?: any): Promise<void> {
    if (this.isUpdatingL1Fees) return;
    this.isUpdatingL1Fees = true;

    try {
      // 1. Base GasPriceOracle
      if (providerBase) {
        try {
          const oracleAbi = [
            'function l1BaseFee() view returns (uint256)',
            'function blobBaseFee() view returns (uint256)',
            'function baseFeeScalar() view returns (uint32)',
            'function blobBaseFeeScalar() view returns (uint32)'
          ];
          const oracle = new (providerBase.constructor as any) ? (providerBase as any) : null;
          // Si providerBase es un JsonRpcProvider o similar
          const ethersModule = await import('ethers');
          const contract = new ethersModule.Contract('0x420000000000000000000000000000000000000F', oracleAbi, providerBase);
          const [l1BaseFee, blobBaseFee, baseFeeScalar, blobBaseFeeScalar] = await Promise.all([
            contract.l1BaseFee().catch(() => null),
            contract.blobBaseFee().catch(() => null),
            contract.baseFeeScalar().catch(() => null),
            contract.blobBaseFeeScalar().catch(() => null)
          ]);

          if (l1BaseFee != null) this.liveL1BaseFeeBase = BigInt(l1BaseFee);
          if (blobBaseFee != null) this.liveBlobBaseFeeBase = BigInt(blobBaseFee);
          if (baseFeeScalar != null) this.liveBaseFeeScalarBase = Number(baseFeeScalar);
          if (blobBaseFeeScalar != null) this.liveBlobBaseFeeScalarBase = Number(blobBaseFeeScalar);
        } catch {}
      }

      // 2. Arbitrum ArbGasInfo
      if (providerArb) {
        try {
          const arbAbi = ['function getL1BaseFeeEstimate() view returns (uint256)'];
          const ethersModule = await import('ethers');
          const arbContract = new ethersModule.Contract('0x000000000000000000000000000000000000006C', arbAbi, providerArb);
          const arbL1Fee = await arbContract.getL1BaseFeeEstimate().catch(() => null);
          if (arbL1Fee != null) this.liveL1BaseFeeArb = BigInt(arbL1Fee);
        } catch {}
      }

      this.lastL1UpdateTimestamp = Date.now();
    } catch {} finally {
      this.isUpdatingL1Fees = false;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Cálculo de Gas Real en L2: Suma de L2 Execution Fee + L1 Calldata/Blob Fee (OP Stack / Arbitrum Nitro)
   * Integra la fórmula EIP-4844 Fjord de Base y ArbGasInfo en Arbitrum.
   */
  public static calculateTotalGasCostUSD(
    chainId: number,
    gasUsedL2: bigint = 150_000n,
    l2GasPriceGwei: number = 0.05,
    ethPriceUSD: number = 2500,
    calldataSizeBytes: number = 384,
    l1BaseFeeGweiOverride?: number
  ): { totalGasUSD: number; l2GasUSD: number; l1DataFeeUSD: number } {
    // 1. Costo de ejecución L2
    const l2GasEth = (Number(gasUsedL2) * l2GasPriceGwei) / 1e9;
    const l2GasUSD = l2GasEth * ethPriceUSD;

    // 2. Costo L1 Data Fee Dinámico (EIP-4844 Blobs + Calldata)
    let l1DataFeeUSD = 0;

    if (chainId === 8453) {
      // Base (OP Stack Fjord / Ecotone scalar con Blobs)
      // Fórmula oficial: l1Fee = (16 * calldataSize * l1BaseFee * baseFeeScalar + calldataSize * blobBaseFee * blobBaseFeeScalar) / (1e6 * 1e9 * 1e9)
      const calldataBig = BigInt(calldataSizeBytes);
      const l1BaseFee = l1BaseFeeGweiOverride != null ? BigInt(Math.round(l1BaseFeeGweiOverride * 1e9)) : this.liveL1BaseFeeBase;
      const blobBaseFee = this.liveBlobBaseFeeBase;

      const basePortion = 16n * calldataBig * l1BaseFee * BigInt(this.liveBaseFeeScalarBase);
      const blobPortion = calldataBig * blobBaseFee * BigInt(this.liveBlobBaseFeeScalarBase);
      const totalL1Wei = (basePortion + blobPortion) / 1000000n; // scalar divisor es 1e6

      const l1FeeEth = Number(totalL1Wei) / 1e18;
      l1DataFeeUSD = Math.max(l1FeeEth * ethPriceUSD, 0.0005);
    } else if (chainId === 42161) {
      // Arbitrum One Nitro L1 calldata poster fee
      const l1BaseFeeWei = l1BaseFeeGweiOverride != null ? BigInt(Math.round(l1BaseFeeGweiOverride * 1e9)) : this.liveL1BaseFeeArb;
      const arbL1FeeWei = BigInt(calldataSizeBytes) * 16n * l1BaseFeeWei;
      const l1FeeEth = Number(arbL1FeeWei) / 1e18;
      l1DataFeeUSD = Math.max(l1FeeEth * ethPriceUSD, 0.0005);
    }

    const totalGasUSD = parseFloat((l2GasUSD + l1DataFeeUSD).toFixed(5));
    return { totalGasUSD, l2GasUSD, l1DataFeeUSD };
  }

  /**
   * Fórmula Analítica Cerrada para Pools Constant Product (x * y = k)
   * Resuelve d(Beneficio)/dL = 0
   */
  static calculateAnalyticalOptimalLoan(
    x1: number,
    y1: number,
    gamma1: number,
    x2: number,
    y2: number,
    gamma2: number
  ): number {
    const numerator = Math.sqrt(x1 * x2 * y1 * y2 * gamma1 * gamma2) - (x1 * y2);
    const denominator = (gamma1 * y2) + (x2 * gamma1 * gamma2);
    if (denominator <= 0 || numerator <= 0) return 0;
    return numerator / denominator;
  }

  /**
   * Calcula el monto óptimo de Flash Loan usando búsqueda ternaria/convexa en memoria RAM (< 0.1ms).
   * Integra el costo real de L1 Data Fee para que nunca apruebe operaciones con margen negativo.
   */
  public static calculateOptimalLoan(
    baseAmountUSD: number,
    spread: number,
    liquidityScore: number = 1.0,
    gasCostUSD: number = 0.05,
    tokenPriceUSD: number = 2500,
    flashLoanFeeRate: number = 0.0, // 0.0 para Balancer/Morpho, 0.0005 para Aave
    minProfitThreshold: number = 0.005,
    chainId: number = 8453
  ): SweetSpotResult {
    // Si no hay spread positivo suficiente para cubrir el gas, no es viable
    if (spread <= 0.0002) {
      return {
        optimalLoanAmount: (baseAmountUSD / tokenPriceUSD).toFixed(6),
        optimalLoanUSD: baseAmountUSD,
        expectedGrossProfitUSD: 0,
        expectedNetProfitUSD: -gasCostUSD,
        priceImpactPercent: 0,
        sweetSpotRatio: 1.0,
        isViable: false
      };
    }

    // Coeficiente de impacto de precio calibrado según liquidez real en USD
    const poolLiquidityUSD = Math.max(liquidityScore * tokenPriceUSD, 50000);
    const k = 0.5 / poolLiquidityUSD;

    // Función de Beneficio Neto: P(x) = x * (spread - k * x - flashLoanFeeRate) - gasCostUSD
    const effectiveSpread = spread - flashLoanFeeRate;
    let optimalLoanUSD = baseAmountUSD;

    if (effectiveSpread > 0 && k > 0) {
      const theoreticalOptimalUSD = effectiveSpread / (2 * k);
      const maxSafeLoan = Math.min(poolLiquidityUSD * 0.10, 100000);
      optimalLoanUSD = Math.max(200, Math.min(theoreticalOptimalUSD, maxSafeLoan));
    }

    // Búsqueda de refinamiento local (Ternary Search)
    let low = optimalLoanUSD * 0.5;
    let high = optimalLoanUSD * 1.5;
    let bestLoanUSD = optimalLoanUSD;
    let maxNetProfit = -Infinity;

    for (let iter = 0; iter < 10; iter++) {
      const mid1 = low + (high - low) / 3;
      const mid2 = high - (high - low) / 3;

      const pnl1 = this.evaluatePnL(mid1, spread, k, flashLoanFeeRate, gasCostUSD);
      const pnl2 = this.evaluatePnL(mid2, spread, k, flashLoanFeeRate, gasCostUSD);

      if (pnl1 < pnl2) {
        low = mid1;
        bestLoanUSD = mid2;
        maxNetProfit = pnl2;
      } else {
        high = mid2;
        bestLoanUSD = mid1;
        maxNetProfit = pnl1;
      }
    }

    const priceImpact = (k * bestLoanUSD) * 100;
    const grossProfit = bestLoanUSD * Math.max(0, spread - (k * bestLoanUSD) - flashLoanFeeRate);
    const netProfit = grossProfit - gasCostUSD;
    const optimalAmountToken = (bestLoanUSD / Math.max(tokenPriceUSD, 0.000001)).toFixed(6);
    const sweetSpotRatio = bestLoanUSD / Math.max(baseAmountUSD, 1);

    return {
      optimalLoanAmount: optimalAmountToken,
      optimalLoanUSD: Math.round(bestLoanUSD),
      expectedGrossProfitUSD: Number(grossProfit.toFixed(2)),
      expectedNetProfitUSD: Number(netProfit.toFixed(2)),
      priceImpactPercent: Number(priceImpact.toFixed(4)),
      sweetSpotRatio: Number(sweetSpotRatio.toFixed(2)),
      isViable: netProfit >= minProfitThreshold && priceImpact < 1.5
    };
  }

  private static evaluatePnL(
    loanUSD: number,
    spread: number,
    k: number,
    feeRate: number,
    gasCostUSD: number
  ): number {
    const netMargin = spread - (k * loanUSD) - feeRate;
    if (netMargin <= 0) return -gasCostUSD;
    return (loanUSD * netMargin) - gasCostUSD;
  }

  /**
   * Búsqueda por Sección Áurea (Golden Section Search) para funciones evaluadoras asíncronas
   */
  static async findOptimalLoan(
    pair: ArbitragePair,
    evaluateFn: (amountStr: string) => Promise<ArbitrageOpportunity | null>
  ): Promise<ArbitrageOpportunity | null> {
    const baseAmount = parseFloat(pair.defaultLoanAmount);
    if (baseAmount <= 0) return null;

    const probeMultipliers = [0.5, 1.0, 2.5, 5.0];
    const probePromises = probeMultipliers.map(async (m) => {
      const amt = (baseAmount * m).toFixed(6);
      const opp = await evaluateFn(amt).catch(() => null);
      return { multiplier: m, amountStr: amt, opp };
    });

    const probeResults = await Promise.all(probePromises);
    const validProbes = probeResults.filter((p): p is { multiplier: number; amountStr: string; opp: ArbitrageOpportunity } => p.opp !== null);

    if (validProbes.length === 0) return null;

    validProbes.sort((a, b) => b.opp.netProfitUSD - a.opp.netProfitUSD);
    const bestInitial = validProbes[0];

    if (bestInitial.opp.netProfitUSD <= 0) {
      return bestInitial.opp;
    }

    let a = Math.max(0.1, bestInitial.multiplier * 0.6);
    let b = bestInitial.multiplier * 1.5;
    const tolerance = 0.05;

    let c = b - (b - a) * this.RES_PHI;
    let d = a + (b - a) * this.RES_PHI;

    let oppC = await evaluateFn((baseAmount * c).toFixed(6)).catch(() => null);
    let oppD = await evaluateFn((baseAmount * d).toFixed(6)).catch(() => null);

    let profitC = oppC ? oppC.netProfitUSD : -Infinity;
    let profitD = oppD ? oppD.netProfitUSD : -Infinity;

    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      if (Math.abs(b - a) < tolerance) break;

      if (profitC > profitD) {
        b = d;
        d = c;
        profitD = profitC;
        oppD = oppC;
        c = b - (b - a) * this.RES_PHI;
        oppC = await evaluateFn((baseAmount * c).toFixed(6)).catch(() => null);
        profitC = oppC ? oppC.netProfitUSD : -Infinity;
      } else {
        a = c;
        c = d;
        profitC = profitD;
        oppC = oppD;
        d = a + (b - a) * this.RES_PHI;
        oppD = await evaluateFn((baseAmount * d).toFixed(6)).catch(() => null);
        profitD = oppD ? oppD.netProfitUSD : -Infinity;
      }
    }

    const finalMultiplier = (a + b) / 2;
    const finalAmountStr = (baseAmount * finalMultiplier).toFixed(6);
    const finalOpp = await evaluateFn(finalAmountStr).catch(() => null);

    if (finalOpp && finalOpp.netProfitUSD > bestInitial.opp.netProfitUSD) {
      finalOpp.sweetSpotRatio = finalMultiplier;
      return finalOpp;
    }

    bestInitial.opp.sweetSpotRatio = bestInitial.multiplier;
    return bestInitial.opp;
  }
}

import { ethers } from 'ethers';
import { ENV } from '../config/env';
import { SweetSpotOptimizer } from './sweetSpotOptimizer';
import { flashLoanProviderService } from '../services/flashLoanProviderService';

export interface EconomicCalculationParams {
  chainId: number;
  loanTokenAddress: string;
  loanAmount: bigint;
  simulatedGrossProfitUnits: bigint;
  tokenPriceUSD: number;
  ethPriceUSD: number;
  gasEstimatedL2?: bigint;
  l2GasPriceGwei?: number;
  calldataSizeBytes?: number;
  slippageBufferBps?: number; // e.g. 15 bps = 0.15%
}

export interface EconomicEvaluationResult {
  tokenDecimals: number;
  loanTokenSymbol: string;
  loanAmountFormatted: string;
  loanAmountUSD: number;
  grossProfitFormatted: string;
  grossProfitUSD: number;
  l2GasUSD: number;
  l1DataFeeUSD: number;
  totalGasCostUSD: number;
  totalGasCostETH: number;
  slippageBufferUSD: number;
  netProfitUSD: number;
  minRequiredProfitUSD: number;
  isProfitable: boolean;
  rejectionReason?: string;
  recommendedContractMinProfitUnits: bigint;
}

export class EconomicMath {
  /**
   * Calcula el minProfit en unidades del token para trasladar al Smart Contract.
   * Garantiza que el contrato inteligente no acepte spreads microscópicos que no cubran
   * los costos de transacción o el umbral mínimo configurado.
   */
  public static calculateContractMinProfit(
    minProfitUSD: number,
    tokenPriceUSD: number,
    tokenDecimals: number
  ): bigint {
    const safeMinUSD = Math.max(minProfitUSD, 0.25);
    const safePrice = Math.max(tokenPriceUSD, 0.00001);
    const requiredTokensFloat = safeMinUSD / safePrice;
    
    try {
      const units = ethers.parseUnits(requiredTokensFloat.toFixed(tokenDecimals > 8 ? 8 : tokenDecimals), tokenDecimals);
      return units > 0n ? units : 1n;
    } catch {
      // Fallback seguro según decimales
      if (tokenDecimals === 6) return 250_000n; // 0.25 USDC
      if (tokenDecimals === 8) return 370n; // ~$0.25 BTC
      return 100_000_000_000_000n; // 0.0001 WETH (~$0.25)
    }
  }

  /**
   * Evaluación económica integral y rigurosa:
   * Beneficio Neto USD = Retorno Bruto - Préstamo - Comisión Flash - Gas L2 - L1 Data Fee - Margen de Seguridad
   */
  public static evaluateOpportunity(params: EconomicCalculationParams): EconomicEvaluationResult {
    const tokenDecimals = flashLoanProviderService.getStrictDecimals(params.loanTokenAddress, 18);
    const loanTokenSymbol = tokenDecimals === 6 ? 'USDC' : (tokenDecimals === 8 ? 'cbBTC' : 'WETH');

    const loanAmountFormatted = ethers.formatUnits(params.loanAmount, tokenDecimals);
    const loanAmountNum = parseFloat(loanAmountFormatted);
    const loanAmountUSD = loanAmountNum * params.tokenPriceUSD;

    const grossProfitFormatted = ethers.formatUnits(params.simulatedGrossProfitUnits, tokenDecimals);
    const grossProfitTokens = parseFloat(grossProfitFormatted);
    const grossProfitUSD = parseFloat((grossProfitTokens * params.tokenPriceUSD).toFixed(6));

    // Gas Real L2 + L1 Data Fee
    const gasUnits = params.gasEstimatedL2 || 220_000n;
    const l2Gwei = params.l2GasPriceGwei || 0.05;
    const calldataBytes = params.calldataSizeBytes || 384;

    const gasBreakdown = SweetSpotOptimizer.calculateTotalGasCostUSD(
      params.chainId,
      gasUnits,
      l2Gwei,
      params.ethPriceUSD,
      calldataBytes
    );

    const totalGasCostUSD = gasBreakdown.totalGasUSD;
    const totalGasCostETH = params.ethPriceUSD > 0 ? totalGasCostUSD / params.ethPriceUSD : 0;

    // Margen de seguridad por slippage / movimiento de reservas (15 bps = 0.15% del préstamo, o 15% del margen bruto)
    // El margen de seguridad evita transacciones con spreads tan finos que un swap mínimo previo las haga revertir.
    const slippageBufferBps = params.slippageBufferBps ?? 15;
    const loanSlippageUSD = (loanAmountUSD * slippageBufferBps) / 10_000;
    const profitHaircutUSD = grossProfitUSD * 0.10; // 10% de amortiguación sobre ganancia bruta
    const slippageBufferUSD = parseFloat(Math.min(loanSlippageUSD, profitHaircutUSD).toFixed(6));

    const netProfitUSD = parseFloat((grossProfitUSD - totalGasCostUSD - slippageBufferUSD).toFixed(6));
    const minRequiredProfitUSD = Math.max(ENV.minProfitUsd, 0.25);

    let isProfitable = false;
    let rejectionReason: string | undefined;

    if (grossProfitUSD <= 0) {
      rejectionReason = 'Gross profit is zero or negative';
    } else if (grossProfitUSD <= totalGasCostUSD) {
      rejectionReason = `Gross profit ($${grossProfitUSD}) does not cover total gas ($${totalGasCostUSD})`;
    } else if (netProfitUSD < minRequiredProfitUSD) {
      rejectionReason = `Net profit ($${netProfitUSD}) below minimum threshold ($${minRequiredProfitUSD})`;
    } else {
      isProfitable = true;
    }

    const recommendedContractMinProfitUnits = this.calculateContractMinProfit(
      minRequiredProfitUSD,
      params.tokenPriceUSD,
      tokenDecimals
    );

    return {
      tokenDecimals,
      loanTokenSymbol,
      loanAmountFormatted,
      loanAmountUSD,
      grossProfitFormatted,
      grossProfitUSD,
      l2GasUSD: gasBreakdown.l2GasUSD,
      l1DataFeeUSD: gasBreakdown.l1DataFeeUSD,
      totalGasCostUSD,
      totalGasCostETH,
      slippageBufferUSD,
      netProfitUSD: Math.max(0, netProfitUSD),
      minRequiredProfitUSD,
      isProfitable,
      rejectionReason,
      recommendedContractMinProfitUnits,
    };
  }
}

import { ethers } from 'ethers';

  feeBps: number;
  feeAmount: bigint;
  availableLiquidity: bigint;
  reason: string;
}

  loanAmount: bigint;
  provider: 'balancer' | 'aave';
  decimals: number;
  tokenPriceUSD: number;

  totalOwed: bigint;

// Known decimals map to prevent erroneous 18-decimal assumptions
export const KNOWN_TOKEN_DECIMALS: Record<string, number> = {
  // Base (8453)
  '0x4200000000000000000000000000000000000006': 18, // WETH
  '0x833589fcd6edb6e08f4c7c32d4f71b54bda02913': 6,  // USDC

  '0x0555e30da8f98308edb960aa94c0db47230d2b9c': 8,  // WBTC (8 decimals!)
  '0x940181a94a35a4569e4529a3cdfb74e38fd98631': 18, // AERO
  '0xc1cba3fcea344f92d9239c08c0568f6f2f0ee452': 18, // wstETH
  '0x2416092f143378750bb29b79ed961ab195cceea5': 18, // ezETH

  // Arbitrum (42161)
  '0x82af49447d8a07e3bd95bd0d56f35241523fbab1': 18, // WETH
  '0xaf88d065e77c8cc2239327c5edb3a432268e5831': 6,  // USDC
  '0xff970a61a04b1ca14834a43f5de4533ebddb5cc8': 6,  // USDC.e
  '0xfd086bc7cd5c481dcc9c85ebe478a1c0b69fcbb9': 6,  // USDT
  '0x2f2a2543b76a4166549f7aab2e75bef0aefc5b0f': 8,  // WBTC
};

export const KNOWN_ATOKEN_ADDRESSES: Record<string, string> = {
  // Base (8453)
  '0x4200000000000000000000000000000000000006': '0xD4a0e0b9149BCee3C920d2E00b5dE09138fd8bb7', // WETH
  '0x833589fcd6edb6e08f4c7c32d4f71b54bda02913': '0x4e65fE4DbA92FcFE37222824aFa3534E58257C0A', // USDC
  '0xcbb7c0000ab88b473b1f5afd9ef808440eed33bf': '0xc230678d46A08537536dD6806967B71e626e2b9c', // cbBTC

  // Arbitrum (42161)
  '0x82af49447d8a07e3bd95bd0d56f35241523fbab1': '0xe50fA9b3c56FfB159cB0FCA61F5c9D750e8128c8', // WETH
  '0xaf88d065e77c8cc2239327c5edb3a432268e5831': '0x724dc807b04555b71ed48a6896b6F41593b8C637', // USDC
};

export class FlashLoanProviderService {
  private static instance: FlashLoanProviderService;
  private vaultCache: Map<string, { paused: boolean; balance: bigint; timestamp: number }> = new Map();
  private aaveCache: Map<string, { balance: bigint; timestamp: number }> = new Map();
  private readonly CACHE_TTL_MS = 15_000; // 15s cache for fast scanning

  private constructor() {}

  public static getInstance(): FlashLoanProviderService {
    if (!FlashLoanProviderService.instance) {
      FlashLoanProviderService.instance = new FlashLoanProviderService();
    }
    return FlashLoanProviderService.instance;
  }

  /**
   * Obtiene los decimales estrictos de un token
   */
  public getStrictDecimals(tokenAddress: string, fallbackDecimals: number = 18): number {
    const normalized = tokenAddress.toLowerCase();
    if (KNOWN_TOKEN_DECIMALS[normalized] !== undefined) {
      return KNOWN_TOKEN_DECIMALS[normalized];
    }
    return fallbackDecimals;
  }

  /**
   * Parsea un monto respetando estrictamente los decimales (6, 8 o 18)
   */
  public parseTokenUnits(amountStr: string, decimals: number): bigint {
    return ethers.parseUnits(amountStr, decimals);
  }

  /**
   * Formatea un monto respetando estrictamente los decimales
   */
  public formatTokenUnits(amount: bigint | string, decimals: number): string {
    return ethers.formatUnits(amount, decimals);
  }

  /**
   * Calcula la comisión exacta del flash loan según el proveedor
   * Balancer V2 = 0 bps (0.00%)
   * Aave V3 = 5 bps (0.05%), división con redondeo hacia arriba
   */
  public computeFlashFee(loanAmount: bigint, provider: 'balancer' | 'aave'): bigint {
    if (provider === 'balancer') {
      return 0n;
    }
    // Aave V3: 5 bps (0.05%). Redondeo hacia arriba para proteger contra insuficiencia de repago
    return (loanAmount * 5n + 9999n) / 10000n;
  }

  /**
   * Consulta el estado del Vault de Balancer V2 (Pausa y Liquidez del Token)
   */
  public async getBalancerVaultLiquidity(
    chainId: number,
    tokenAddress: string,
    provider: ethers.Provider
  ): Promise<{ paused: boolean; balance: bigint }> {
    const vaultAddress = BALANCER_VAULT_ADDRESS[chainId];
    if (!vaultAddress) {
      return { paused: true, balance: 0n };
    }

    const cacheKey = `${chainId}-${tokenAddress.toLowerCase()}`;
    const cached = this.vaultCache.get(cacheKey);
    if (cached && Date.now() - cached.timestamp < this.CACHE_TTL_MS) {
      return { paused: cached.paused, balance: cached.balance };
    }

    try {
      const vaultContract = new ethers.Contract(
        vaultAddress,
        ['function getPausedState() external view returns (bool paused, uint256 pauseWindowEndTime, uint256 bufferPeriodEndTime)'],
        provider
      );
      const tokenContract = new ethers.Contract(
        tokenAddress,
        ['function balanceOf(address account) external view returns (uint256)'],
        provider
      );

      const timeoutPromise = new Promise<any>((resolve) => setTimeout(() => resolve(null), 12000));
      const [pausedRes, balRes] = await Promise.all([
        Promise.race([vaultContract.getPausedState().catch(() => [false]), timeoutPromise]),
        Promise.race([tokenContract.balanceOf(vaultAddress).catch(() => 0n), timeoutPromise]),
      ]);

      const isPaused = Boolean(pausedRes && pausedRes[0] ? pausedRes[0] : false);
      const bal = balRes !== null && balRes !== undefined ? BigInt(balRes) : 0n;

      this.vaultCache.set(cacheKey, { paused: isPaused, balance: bal, timestamp: Date.now() });
      return { paused: isPaused, balance: bal };
    } catch (err: any) {
      logger.warn(`[FLASH LOAN SERVICE] Error consultando Balancer Vault: ${err.message}`);
      return { paused: false, balance: 0n };
    }
  }

  /**
   * Consulta la liquidez disponible en Aave V3 Pool con mapeo rápido de aTokens y timeout
   */
  public async getAaveV3Liquidity(
    chainId: number,
    tokenAddress: string,
    provider: ethers.Provider
  ): Promise<bigint> {
    const poolAddress = AAVE_V3_POOL_ADDRESS[chainId];
    if (!poolAddress) return 0n;

    const cacheKey = `${chainId}-${tokenAddress.toLowerCase()}`;
    const cached = this.aaveCache.get(cacheKey);
    if (cached && Date.now() - cached.timestamp < this.CACHE_TTL_MS) {
      return cached.balance;
    }

    const tokenContract = new ethers.Contract(
      tokenAddress,
      ['function balanceOf(address account) external view returns (uint256)'],
      provider
    );

    const knownAToken = KNOWN_ATOKEN_ADDRESSES[tokenAddress.toLowerCase()];
    if (knownAToken) {
      try {
        const balPromise = tokenContract.balanceOf(knownAToken);
        const timeoutPromise = new Promise<bigint>((resolve) => setTimeout(() => resolve(0n), 12000));
        const bal = BigInt(await Promise.race([balPromise, timeoutPromise]));
        if (bal > 0n) {
          this.aaveCache.set(cacheKey, { balance: bal, timestamp: Date.now() });
          return bal;
        }
      } catch {}
    }

    try {
      const poolContract = new ethers.Contract(
        poolAddress,
        ['function getReserveData(address asset) external view returns (tuple(uint256 configuration, uint128 liquidityIndex, uint128 currentLiquidityRate, uint128 variableBorrowIndex, uint128 currentVariableBorrowRate, uint128 currentStableBorrowRate, uint40 lastUpdateTimestamp, uint16 id, address aTokenAddress, address stableDebtTokenAddress, address variableDebtTokenAddress, address interestRateStrategyAddress, uint128 accruedToTreasury, uint128 unbacked, uint128 isolationModeTotalDebt))'],
        provider
      );

      const rdPromise = poolContract.getReserveData(tokenAddress);
      const timeoutPromise = new Promise<any>((_, reject) => setTimeout(() => reject(new Error('timeout')), 12000));
      const reserveData = await Promise.race([rdPromise, timeoutPromise]);
      const aTokenAddress = reserveData[8];
      const bal = BigInt(await tokenContract.balanceOf(aTokenAddress));

      this.aaveCache.set(cacheKey, { balance: bal, timestamp: Date.now() });
      return bal;
    } catch {
      // Fallback seguro: saldo nominal
      return 10_000_000_000_000_000_000n; // > 0
    }
  }

  /**
   * Selección Dinámica de Proveedor de Flash Loan con Comprobación On-Chain
   * 1. Si Balancer V2 no está pausado y tiene saldo >= requestedAmount -> Selecciona Balancer (0% comisión).
   * 2. Si no, deriva automáticamente a Aave V3 calculando la comisión de 5 bps.
   */
  public async selectOptimalProvider(
    chainId: number,
    tokenAddress: string,
    requestedAmount: bigint,
    provider: ethers.Provider
  ): Promise<FlashLoanProviderInfo> {
    const balancerStatus = await this.getBalancerVaultLiquidity(chainId, tokenAddress, provider);

    if (!balancerStatus.paused && balancerStatus.balance >= requestedAmount) {
      return {
        provider: 'balancer',
        feeBps: 0,
        feeAmount: 0n,
        availableLiquidity: balancerStatus.balance,
        reason: `Balancer V2 disponible con 0% de comisión (Saldo Vault: ${balancerStatus.balance.toString()})`,
      };
    }

    // Fallback a Aave V3
    const aaveLiquidity = await this.getAaveV3Liquidity(chainId, tokenAddress, provider);
    const aaveFee = this.computeFlashFee(requestedAmount, 'aave');

    return {
      provider: 'aave',
      feeBps: 5,
      feeAmount: aaveFee,
      availableLiquidity: aaveLiquidity,
      reason: balancerStatus.paused
        ? `Balancer Vault en pausa. Usando Aave V3 (5 bps de comisión).`
        : `Liquidez insuficiente en Balancer (${balancerStatus.balance.toString()} < ${requestedAmount.toString()}). Usando Aave V3.`,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Desglose económico completo y cálculo del beneficio neto real
   * Deduce préstamo, comisión de flash loan y gas total estimado
   */
  public calculateNetProfit(params: ProfitCalculationParams): ProfitBreakdown {
    const { grossReturn, loanAmount, provider, decimals, tokenPriceUSD, gasCostUSD } = params;

    const flashFee = this.computeFlashFee(loanAmount, provider);
    const totalOwed = loanAmount + flashFee;

    if (grossReturn <= totalOwed) {
      return {
        loanAmount,
        flashFee,
        totalOwed,
        grossReturn,
        grossProfitWei: 0n,
        grossProfitUSD: 0,
        gasCostUSD,
        netProfitUSD: 0,
        isProfitable: false,
      };
    }

    const grossProfitWei = grossReturn - totalOwed;
    const grossProfitTokens = parseFloat(ethers.formatUnits(grossProfitWei, decimals));
    const grossProfitUSD = parseFloat((grossProfitTokens * tokenPriceUSD).toFixed(4));
    const netProfitUSD = Math.max(0, parseFloat((grossProfitUSD - gasCostUSD).toFixed(4)));

    return {
      loanAmount,
      flashFee,
      totalOwed,
      grossReturn,
      grossProfitWei,
      grossProfitUSD,
      gasCostUSD,
      netProfitUSD,
      isProfitable: netProfitUSD > 0,
    };
  }
}

export const flashLoanProviderService = FlashLoanProviderService.getInstance();

import { ethers } from 'ethers';
import { ENV } from '../config/env';
import { logger } from '../utils/logger';
import { executionLedger } from '../utils/executionLedger';
import { SERVER_STATS, addScanLog } from '../server';
import { savePersistedStats } from '../utils/statsStore';
import { EconomicMath, EconomicEvaluationResult } from '../math/economicMath';

export interface DispatchSpatialParams {
  chainId: number;
  pairName: string;
  provider: 'balancer' | 'aave';
  contract: ethers.Contract;
  routeStruct: {
    tokenIn: string;
    tokenIntermediate: string;
    dexBuy: number;
    dexSell: number;
    feeOrSpacingBuy: number;
    feeOrSpacingSell: number;
    minProfit: bigint;
  };
